CREATE TABLE "NMG"."asmz"
(
"KQ001" VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
"AAA001" VARCHAR(50),
"AAB001" VARCHAR(50),
"BZX001" VARCHAR(50),
"AAC002" VARCHAR(30),
"AAC003" VARCHAR(300),
"IOX008" VARCHAR(10),
"KQSJ" DATETIME(0),
"KQDD" VARCHAR(100),
"AAB301" VARCHAR(12),
"AAC009" BLOB,
"AAC200" VARCHAR(100),
"AAC201" VARCHAR(5),
"AAC202" DECIMAL(10,6),
"AAC203" DECIMAL(10,6),
"AAC204" DECIMAL(3,1),
"STATUS" VARCHAR(2),
"ISVALID" VARCHAR(2),
"KQSTATE" VARCHAR(2),
"ISLOOK" VARCHAR(2),
"EXPLAIN" VARCHAR(150),
"aac001" VARCHAR(50),
"KQRQ" DATE,
"DA001" VARCHAR2(64),
"SJLY" VARCHAR2(2),
"YCYY" VARCHAR(500),
"NORMAL" VARCHAR(2),
"BATCHNO" VARCHAR(50),
"AAE039" VARCHAR(20),
"integritycode" TEXT,
NOT CLUSTER PRIMARY KEY("KQ001")) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."asmz" IS '住建考勤记录表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAA001" IS '项目id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAB001" IS '分包单位id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAB301" IS '行政区划代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."aac001" IS '人员主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC002" IS '姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC003" IS '证件号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC009" IS '刷卡近照。Base64字符串，不超过50KB';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC200" IS '通道的名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC201" IS '考勤方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC202" IS '考勤所在经度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC203" IS '考勤所在纬度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAC204" IS '务工人员体温';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."AAE039" IS '考勤年月';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."BATCHNO" IS '批次号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."BZX001" IS '班组id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."DA001" IS '日统计主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."EXPLAIN" IS '说明';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."asmz"."IOX008" IS '入场/出场';

(

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary" IS '疑点数据下载记录总表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."ABE214" IS '数据产生日期，格式YYYYMMDD,默认为空，仅当需要下载特定日期问题数据时填写。';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."BIZSTATUS" IS '数据状态：0-无效；1-有效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."CODE" IS '返回结果码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."CREATEBY" IS '创建者(调用接口者)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."CREATEDATE" IS '创建时间(调用接口时间)';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."MESSAGE" IS '返回数据校验结果详细信息';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."OUTBATCHNUM" IS '交易批次号-当数据获取结束标志（pageFlag）为“0”时，部里提供交易批次号outbatchNum，用于地方再次交易是作为查询条件batchNum，继续获取同批次未下载完成的数据。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."PAGEFLAG" IS '数据获取结束标志 “0”-未结束（存在未获取的数据）“1”-结束（已获取到全部数据）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."REMAIN" IS '剩余未获取记录数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."SUREFLAG" IS '调用数据下载确认接口返回码(与返回码保持一致)：00-业务成功；01-业务部分成功；10-业务失败；20-系统错误；30-签名失败；40-解密失败；90-其他错误';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."TOTAL" IS '本次返回记录数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."by_ret_point_data_summary"."TRANSACTIONID" IS '交易流水号，此流水号要在省级系统保留，用于交易结果确认';

CREATE TABLE "NMG"."bzj_summary"
(
"ABZ106" VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
"AAA001" VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
"ABE174" VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
"ABE165" VARCHAR(1) NOT NULL,
"ABE112" VARCHAR(1) NOT NULL,
"ABB128" VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
"ABB132" VARCHAR(100),
"ABB133" VARCHAR(40),
"ABE205" DECIMAL(16,2),
"ABE124" VARCHAR(1) NOT NULL,
"ABE166" DATE NOT NULL,
"ABB131" DECIMAL(16,2),
"ABB129" DECIMAL(16,2) NOT NULL,
"ABE204" DECIMAL(16,2) NOT NULL,
"ABB136" VARCHAR(100),
"ABB170" VARCHAR(60),
"ABE206" DECIMAL(16,2),
"ABB138" DATE,
"ABB139" DATE,
"ABB164" VARCHAR(150),
"ABB171" VARCHAR(60),
"ABE207" DECIMAL(16,2),
"ABB165" DATE,
"ABB166" DATE,
"ABB167" VARCHAR(150),
"ABB172" VARCHAR(40),
"ABE208" DECIMAL(16,2),
"ABB168" DATE,
"ABB169" DATE,
"ABB143" DECIMAL(16,2),
"ABB144" DATE,
"AAE091" TIMESTAMP(6),
"ABE301" DECIMAL(16,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS ((((( CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE205 IS NULL ) THEN ABE205 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END  +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE206 IS NULL ) THEN ABE206 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END ) +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE207 IS NULL ) THEN ABE207 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END ) +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE208 IS NULL ) THEN ABE208 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END ) +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABB143 IS NULL ) THEN ABB143 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END )) VIRTUAL ,
NOT CLUSTER PRIMARY KEY("ABZ106")) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."bzj_summary" IS '工资保证金账户收支信息表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."AAA001" IS '项目编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."AAE091" IS '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB128" IS '收支方式,可多选，同时存在多种收支方式时，以半角逗号“,”作为分隔符，不在代码表中的，不允许入库';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB129" IS '核定金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB131" IS '差异化存储金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB132" IS '工资保证金开户银行：指工资保证金开户的银行名称，当“缴纳方式”为“现金”或“银行保函”时必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB133" IS '工资保证金银行账号：指工资保证金的银行账号，当“缴纳方式”为“现金”或“银行保函”时必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB136" IS '担保银行名称：担保银行名称，当缴纳方式包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB138" IS '银行保函有效期起始日期，格式YYYYMMDD，当缴纳方式包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB139" IS '银行保函有效期终止日期，格式YYYYMMDD，当缴纳方式包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB143" IS '其他金额,指本次收支的其他金额，单位为元';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB144" IS '收支时间：保证金账户收支变动时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB164" IS '工程担保公司名称：指提供工程担保单位的名称，当缴纳方式包含“工程担保公司保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB165" IS '工程担保公司保函有效起始日期：指工程担保公司保函的有效起始日期，当缴纳方式包含“工程担保公司保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB166" IS '工程担保公司保函有效终止日期：指工程担保公司保函的有效终止日期，当缴纳方式包含“工程担保公司保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB167" IS '保险公司名称：指提供工程保证保险的保险公司名称，当缴纳方式包含“工程保证保险”时必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB168" IS '保险有效期起始日期：指工程保证保险有效期起始日期，当缴纳方式包含“工程保证保险”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB169" IS '保险有效期终止日期：指工程保证保险有效期终止日期，格式：YYYYMMDD，日期格式不符的，不允许入库；当缴纳方式包含“工程保证保险”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB170" IS '银行保函函号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB171" IS '工程担保公司保函函号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABB172" IS '保险单号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE112" IS '交易类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE124" IS '存储类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE165" IS '收支类型：1-进账，2-出账';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE166" IS '存储起始日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE174" IS '收支编号：指工资保证金账户收支的唯一编号。保证金每一笔进账记录为一个收支编号；保证金每一笔出账记为一个收支编号（一笔出账可包含多个人员的工资支付）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE204" IS '已存储金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE205" IS '现金金额，指本次收支的工资保证金现金金额，单位为元。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE206" IS '银行保函担保金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE207" IS '工程担保公司担保金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE208" IS '保单金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABE301" IS '保证金存储余额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."bzj_summary"."ABZ106" IS '保证金账户入账ID';


CREATE  INDEX "bzj_summary_AAA001_IDX" ON "NMG"."bzj_summary"("AAA001" ASC) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

CREATE TABLE "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"
(
"PJAS001" VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

"bank_account_no" VARCHAR(400),

"ie_type" INT,
"trn_type" INT,

"pd_deposit_amt" NUMBER(22,0),
"dueofpay" NUMBER(22,0),
"paid_in" NUMBER(22,0),
"ie_method" VARCHAR(200),
"ie_bank_account_no" VARCHAR(400),
"ie_bank_account_name" VARCHAR(400),
"cash_deposit_amt" NUMBER(22,0),
"ABB136" VARCHAR(100),
"blg_code" VARCHAR(200),
"blg_amt" NUMBER(22,0),
"blg_start_date" DATE,
"blg_end_date" DATE,
"other_amt" NUMBER(22,0),
"ie_time" DATETIME(0) NOT NULL,
"bkid" VARCHAR(500),
NOT CLUSTER PRIMARY KEY("PJAS001")) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."projectpromisesalary" IS '工程建设项目保证金账户收支记录';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."ABB136" IS '担保银行名称，当“收支方式”包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."bank_account_no" IS '银行账户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."bkid" IS '银行ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."blg_code" IS '银行保函函号，当“收支方式”包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."blg_end_date" IS '银行保函有效期终止日期，当“收支方式”包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."blg_start_date" IS '银行保函有效期起始日期，当“收支方式”包含“银行保函”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."cash_deposit_amt" IS '缴存现金金额，当“收支方式”包含“现金”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."deposit_start_time" IS '指决定按当前缴存类型进行缴存的时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."deposit_type" IS '指保证金账户的差异化缴存类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."dueofpay" IS '保证金应缴金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."ie_bank_account_name" IS '工资保证金开户的银行账户名称，当收支
方式包括现金时，必须填写';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."ie_bank_account_no" IS '工资保证金银行账号，指工资保证金开户的银行名称，当“收支方式”包含“现金”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."ie_method" IS '收支方式';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."ie_time" IS '收支时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."ie_type" IS '收支类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."other_amt" IS '其他金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."paid_in" IS '指本次缴纳之前已经缴纳的保证金金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."pd_deposit_amt" IS '指缴纳保证金时，因差异化缴存而免除、减少、缓缴或增加的保证金金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."PJAS001" IS '主键ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."serial_number" IS '指账户收支对应的银行流水号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."signcode" IS '银行签约码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."trn_type" IS '交易类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."projectpromisesalary"."unique_code" IS '人社平台项目唯一标识编号';

CREATE TABLE "NMG"."RCJLJ"
(
"NAME" VARCHAR2(100),
"PHONE" VARCHAR2(100),
"IDENTIFICATION" VARCHAR2(100),
"HOUSEHOLD" VARCHAR2(100),
"UNIVERSITY" VARCHAR2(100),
"EDUCATION" VARCHAR2(100),
"DECLAREDAMOUNT" VARCHAR2(100),
"GRADUATIONTIME" VARCHAR2(100),
"EMPLOYMENT" VARCHAR2(100),
"JYTYPE" VARCHAR2(100),
"WORK" VARCHAR2(100),
"SOCIALID" VARCHAR2(100),
"SBTYPE" VARCHAR2(100),
"BANK" VARCHAR2(100),
"BANKBRANCH" VARCHAR2(100),
"BANKNUMBER" VARCHAR2(100),
"ID" VARCHAR2(100),
"BUSINESSID" VARCHAR2(100)) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."RCJLJ" IS '青年人才奖励金奖申报';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."BANK" IS '开户银行';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."EMPLOYMENT" IS '是否已就业';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."HOUSEHOLD" IS '户籍';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."IDENTIFICATION" IS '身份证号码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."JYTYPE" IS '就业创业类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."NAME" IS '申请人姓名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."PHONE" IS '联系电话';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."SBTYPE" IS '申报类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."SOCIALID" IS '统一社会信用代码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."UNIVERSITY" IS '毕业院校';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."RCJLJ"."WORK" IS '单位名称';

CREATE TABLE "NMG"."salarys"
(
"EXTERNAL_BATCH_NO" VARCHAR2(45) NOT NULL,
"CODES" VARCHAR2(25) NOT NULL,
"SIGNCODE" VARCHAR2(45) NOT NULL,
"BKID" VARCHAR2(255) NOT NULL,
"BANK_ACCOUNT_NO" VARCHAR2(40) NOT NULL,
"YEAR_MONTH" VARCHAR2(20) NOT NULL,
"AMTSUM" DECIMAL(16,2) NOT NULL,
"COUNTS" VARCHAR2(255) NOT NULL,
"STATUS" VARCHAR2(2) NOT NULL,
"TJTIME" DATETIME(6),
"DFTIME" DATETIME(6),
"QRTIME" DATETIME(6),
"BATCH_NO" VARCHAR2(30),
"ZFBZ" VARCHAR2(1),
"TELEPHONE" VARCHAR2(11)) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."salarys" IS '代发工资申请总表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."AMTSUM" IS '代发总金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."BANK_ACCOUNT_NO" IS '银行账户';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."BATCH_NO" IS '平台银行代发批次号（接口返回）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."BKID" IS '银行账户唯一编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."CODES" IS '三方系统项目唯一标识编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."COUNTS" IS '代发总记录数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."DFTIME" IS '代发申请时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."EXTERNAL_BATCH_NO" IS '三方唯一批次编号（使用SEQ_SALARYS_ID序列发生器）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."QRTIME" IS '短信确认时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."SIGNCODE" IS '银行签约码（项目编号）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."STATUS" IS '发放状态（1已提交2已代发申请3已代发确认4银行处理中5部分成功6成功）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."TELEPHONE" IS '签约资金拨付手机号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."TJTIME" IS '提交时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."YEAR_MONTH" IS '发放所属年月';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."salarys"."ZFBZ" IS '支付标志（1首次支付2二次支付）';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."sc_by57_tmp"."STATE1" IS '保证金退还（需要根据是否已经完工获取数据）是否上报部状态（1待上报、2上报成功、3上报失败、9无需处理）';


CREATE TABLE "NMG"."SC_BY58"
(
"ABZ106" VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,

"ABB129" DECIMAL(16,2) NOT NULL,
"ABE204" DECIMAL(16,2) NOT NULL,

"ABB170" VARCHAR(60),

"ABE301" DECIMAL(16,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS ((((( CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE205 IS NULL ) THEN ABE205 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END  +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE206 IS NULL ) THEN ABE206 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END ) +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE207 IS NULL ) THEN ABE207 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END ) +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABE208 IS NULL ) THEN ABE208 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END ) +  CASE  WHEN  NOT (ABB143 IS NULL ) THEN ABB143 WHEN  NOT ( FALSE ) THEN 0 ELSE NULL  END )) VIRTUAL ) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."AAA167" IS '预警名称，手动预警时预警名称为用户手动填写，其他使用预警规则名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."AAB301" IS '行政区划代码，手动预警非必填，其他必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."AAE100" IS '有效标志；0-无效；1有效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."AAE360" IS '再次核实原因（疑点调查结果审核不通过原因）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."AAF017" IS '所属市';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."AAF018" IS '所属省';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABB100" IS '项目编号，手动预警非必填，其他必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE139" IS '预警来源；1-地方上报数据；2-人社内部数据；3-人社外部数据；4-手动预警（使用预警规则中的预警来源）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE141" IS '产生时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE142" IS '限办时限(下发状态为已下发时必填)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE143" IS '预警状态；0-未下发；1-已下发；2-关闭';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE145" IS '疑点下发人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE146" IS '疑点下发时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE147" IS '疑点办理状态；0-未接收；1-已接收；2-已反馈（默认为空，当疑点下发时变更为未接收；接口调用下载确认后变更为已接收；调用接口反馈调查结果变更为已反馈）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE151" IS '是否再次核实，1-是；2-否；原上报疑点调查结果经部级审核不通过，需重新进行调查反馈';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE167" IS '产生疑点数据所属年月，手动预警默认当前年月，手动预警非必填，其他必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE168" IS '预警描述，继承规则中问题描述，手动预警可自行修改';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE172" IS '监测预警等级；1-关注；2-低；3-中；4-高';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE181" IS '是否再处置，1-是；2-否；原上报欠薪疑点的处置结果经部级审核不通过，需重新进行处置反馈';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE182" IS '再次处置原因（疑点处置结果审核不通过原因）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE183" IS '违反规则具体描述，规则执行产生，不下发地方';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE184" IS '欠薪处置反馈状态，0-未反馈，1-已反馈。默认为空，当反馈调查结果“是否发生欠薪”为“是”时，更新为“未反馈”，欠薪处置结果反馈后变更为“已反馈”，“是否再次处置”变更为“否”。欠薪处置结果审核不通过时，变更为未反馈，是否再次处置变更为“是”';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE194" IS '疑点来源：1-部平台规则预警；2-部平台人工预警';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABE214" IS '产生日期，疑点数据产生日期，格式：YYYYMMDD';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABZ101" IS '工程建设项目ID，手动预警非必填，其他必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABZ113" IS '疑点编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABZ120" IS '预警规则ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."ABZ121" IS '规则配置ID，手动预警非必填，其他必填';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."BATCH_ID" IS '疑点生成批次号（规则引擎生成和手动导入共用同一序列batch_id）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."CHECK_RESULT" IS '疑点校验结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."CONFIRMSTATUS" IS '下载确认标识：0-未确认；1已确认';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."CONFIRMTIME" IS '下载确认时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."CREATEDATE" IS '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."GROUP_ID" IS '创建机构ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."HASHCODE" IS '哈希值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."REMARK" IS '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."UPDATE_DATE" IS '最后上报时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."UPDATE_GROUP_ID" IS '修改机构ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."UPDATE_USER_ID" IS '修改用户ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY68"."USER_ID" IS '创建用户ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY71"."ABE197" IS '下达责令限期整改文号 指欠薪问题的案件办理成效，可多选，以半角逗号“,”作为分隔符，不在代码表中的不允许入库。';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY71"."ABE200" IS '移送公安机关立案侦查文号 指下达行政处罚决定书的文号，可存在多个，以半角逗号“,”作为分隔符，当案件办理成效包含“下达行政处罚决定书”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY71"."ABE201" IS '列入拖欠农民工工资失信联合惩戒对象名单文号 指移送公安机关立案侦查的文号，可存在多个，以半角逗号“,”作为分隔符，当案件办理成效包含“移送公安机关立案侦查”时必填。';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SC_BY71"."ABZ001" IS '地方预警编号 指地方平台预警唯一编号，长度大于20字节，不允许入库。';

"ARCHIVE_ID" VARCHAR2(32),
"DUE_DATE" TIMESTAMP(0),
"ITEM_CODE" VARCHAR2(50),
"SUPERVISE_STATUS" NUMBER(1,0) DEFAULT 1) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON TABLE "NMG"."SMA_RU_TASK" IS '运行时任务信息';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SMA_RU_TASK"."PROC_DEF_ID" IS '流程定义ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SMA_RU_TASK"."PROC_DEF_KEY" IS '流程定义KEY';

"RULENAME" VARCHAR2(80),
"DETAILE" VARCHAR2(400),
"RULETYPE" VARCHAR2(6),
"CREATER" VARCHAR2(40),
"ACTIVE" VARCHAR2(2),
"ENAME" VARCHAR2(80),
"INPUT" VARCHAR2(400),
"OUTPUT" VARCHAR2(400),
"INDEXFLAG" VARCHAR2(4),
"ISCREATE" VARCHAR2(4),
"RULEPATH" VARCHAR2(4000)) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;

COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."ACTIVE" IS '是否有效';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."CREATER" IS '创建者';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."CREATETIME" IS '创建时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."DETAILE" IS '详细描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."ENAME" IS '英文名，用于创建目录、规则';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."INDEXFLAG" IS '是否生成索引，0--不生成，1--生成';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."INPUT" IS '输入项,可用于传参完整性校验';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."ISCREATE" IS '代码是否生成文件，0--不生成，1--生成，若为目录，可选则不生成';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."OUTPUT" IS '输出项，保留项，目前没用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."PARENTID" IS '父节点ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."RULEID" IS '规则id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."RULENAME" IS '规则名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."RULEPATH" IS '规则路径（由于是否生成文件，导致该字段可能无法使用，预留）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."RULETYPE" IS '规则类型（如1.目录，2.规则--每个规则为一个java）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "NMG"."SYSRULE"."UPDATETIME" IS '更新时间';


CREATE  INDEX "SYS_C0012477" ON "NMG"."SYSRULE"("RULEID" ASC) STORAGE(ON "ZJGZJK", CLUSTERBTR) ;
